<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-copilot/for-individuals/termsofuse" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft: Copilot is for entertainment purposes only</span><span class='zh'>微软：Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途</span></a><span class="dom">microsoft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations.</span><span class='zh'>微软在使用条款中明确表示 Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途，界定了其使用范围与局限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations. This stance raises questions about relying on AI for critical coding or decision-making and highlights the need for clear risk disclosures.</span><span class='zh'>微软在使用条款中明确表示 Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途，界定了其使用范围与局限。此说法引发了人们对将其用于关键编码或决策场景的可依赖性以及需披露的风险信息的关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ogirardot.writizzy.com/p/good-software-knows-when-to-stop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Good software knows when to stop</span><span class='zh'>好软件知道何时该停下来</span></a><span class="dom">ogirardot.writizzy.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A piece argues for restraint in software, warning against feature creep and unnecessary complexity.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张软件开发需要克制，反对无休止的功能堆叠与复杂性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A piece argues for restraint in software, warning against feature creep and unnecessary complexity. Focus on reliability, security, and maintainability—letting tools do a few things well rather than many mediocre ones.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张软件开发需要克制，反对无休止的功能堆叠与复杂性。强调以可靠性、安保和可维护性为核心，让工具专注做好几件事，而不是堆砌大量功能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>535</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261561' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>269</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47464818" data-id="47464818" data-tags="Privacy|Legal|AI" data-score="533" data-comments="149" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47464818" data-time="1774078216">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/03/blocking-internet-archive-wont-stop-ai-it-will-erase-webs-historical-record" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blocking Internet Archive Won&#x27;t Stop AI, but Will Erase Web&#x27;s Historical Record</span><span class='zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会阻止 AI，但会抹去网络的历史记录</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Blocking Internet Archive won&#x27;t curb AI development, and models will still train on data from other sources.</span><span class='zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会遏制 AI 发展，模型仍会从其他来源获取数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Blocking Internet Archive won&#x27;t curb AI development, and models will still train on data from other sources. It would erase a vital historical snapshot of the web, harming researchers, journalists, and accountability. Smarter data-use policies are preferable to outright blackouts.</span><span class='zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会遏制 AI 发展，模型仍会从其他来源获取数据。这会抹去网络的宝贵历史快照，损害研究、报道和问责能力。应采用更聪明的数据使用政策，而不是全面封锁。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://research.google/blog/turboquant-redefining-ai-efficiency-with-extreme-compression/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TurboQuant: Redefining AI efficiency with extreme compression</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant：以极端压缩重塑AI效率</span></a><span class="dom">research.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TurboQuant introduces extreme compression techniques to shrink AI models and cut compute needs while preserving accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant 引入极端压缩技术，在保持准确度的同时显著降低模型规模和计算需求，有望降低大规模部署成本并推动边缘设备或端侧推理的普及。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TurboQuant introduces extreme compression techniques to shrink AI models and cut compute needs while preserving accuracy. This approach tests the boundaries of model distillation and quantization, potentially slashing deployment costs and enabling more edge and on-device inference. Trade-offs remain: training compatibility and potential accuracy quirks in edge cases.</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant 引入极端压缩技术，在保持准确度的同时显著降低模型规模和计算需求，有望降低大规模部署成本并推动边缘设备或端侧推理的普及。该方法可能带来训练兼容性与在极端情形下的精度波动等权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mlu-explain.github.io/decision-tree/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decision trees – the unreasonable power of nested decision rules</span><span class='zh'>决策树：嵌套决策规则的强大力量</span></a><span class="dom">mlu-explain.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nested decision rules let a decision tree encode nuanced logic with transparent paths, but depth can make them unwieldy.</span><span class='zh'>嵌套决策规则使决策树能够以透明的路径表达细致的逻辑，但深度增加会使其变得难以维护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nested decision rules let a decision tree encode nuanced logic with transparent paths, but depth can make them unwieldy. With careful structure and pruning, they remain a practical tool for governable, explainable rules even as data-driven models rise.</span><span class='zh'>嵌套决策规则使决策树能够以透明的路径表达细致的逻辑，但深度增加会使其变得难以维护。通过合适的结构和剪枝，它仍然是可解释性强、可治理的规则工具，即便在数据驱动模型盛行时也有用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta renews its commitment to jemalloc, the memory allocator powering its data infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 再次承诺继续优化 jemalloc，这一为数据基础设施提供分配内存的组件在大规模系统中扮演关键角色。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta renews its commitment to jemalloc, the memory allocator powering its data infrastructure. The move signals continued focus on performance, latency, and memory efficiency at scale, with potential upstream contributions benefiting other projects that rely on jemalloc.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 再次承诺继续优化 jemalloc，这一为数据基础设施提供分配内存的组件在大规模系统中扮演关键角色。此举表明 Meta 将持续提升性能与内存效率，并可能向 jemalloc 的开源社区带来贡献，惠及依赖该分配器的其他项目。</span></p>
